## 3D空間(GeoAI工具)中的探測物體 · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- GeoAI 工具箱
- 3D AI 工具

**english**　Detect Objects In 3D Space

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geoai/detect-objects-in-3d-space.html

**id**　731703dd74e4270460-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geoai/detect-objects-in-3d-space.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Detect Objects In 3D Space，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入面向圖像圖像的資料集 [in_oriented_imagery_dataset]、輸出檢測對象 [out_detected_objects]、輸入定義示范文件 [in_model_definition_file]、參數(備選) [arguments]、感興趣對象(可選) [in_objects_of_interest]、輸入三維層(可選) [in_3d_layer]、輸出深度圖像文件夾( 可選) [out_depth_image_folder]、更新有深度地圖( 可選) 的圖像資料集 [update_depth_image_path]、制止重復探測(可選) [suppress_duplicates]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/geoai/detect-objects-in-3d-space.html#parameters

## 創建本地建模區域(空間統計工具) · 摘要摘要

**id**　73325d70ca8b3c33ce-1

**english**　Create Regions for Local Modeling (SPiRe)

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 模擬空間關系工具軟件

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html

**text**　將點或多邊形特性類分成不同的區域,每個區域都優化,以分別進行局部建模和預測。該工具使用預報制度空間分割建模框架(SPiRe)確定依賴變量與解釋變量之間的關系盡可能一致的區域。更多了解如何為本地建模創建區域(SPire)

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Divides a point or polygon feature class into separate regions that are each optimized for separate local modeling and prediction. The tool uses the Spatial Partitioning for Predictive Regimes (SPiRe) modeling framework to identify regions where the relationships between the dependent and explanatory variables are as consistent as possible.\nLearn more about how Create Regions for Local Modeling (SPiRe) works", "section": "Summary"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html#summary

## 創建本地建模區域(空間統計工具) · 使用率

**id**　73325d70ca8b3c33ce-2

**english**　Create Regions for Local Modeling (SPiRe)

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 模擬空間關系工具軟件

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html

**text**　該工具需要一套解釋變量和從屬變量,其中依附變量將由解釋變量預測。所有變量必須是數字的。該工具將返回一個輸入特征副本,并提供一個區域ID欄位,標明每個特性的區域。這些區域的目的是作為單一全球預測模型的替代物使用。例如,可以使用基于森林和推進回歸和分類工具,利用預測模型中的所有特征預測依賴變量。但是,該模型將假設各變量之間的關系在地圖上是一致的。相反,你可以將資料分為關系盡可能一致的區域。通過在每個區域內建立不同的模型,該工具可以更容易地了解當地特點和關系。這往往導致對關系的更準確預測和更有意義的解釋。 您可以使用“ 分割by 屬性” 工具將區域分為不同的特性類別, 并在 Split Fields 參數中提供區域標識欄位 。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The tool requires a set of explanatory variables and a dependent variables in which the dependent variable will be predicted by the explanatory variables. All variables must be numeric. The tool will return a copy of the input features with a Region ID field indicating the region of each feature.\nThe regions are intended to be used as alternatives to a single global prediction model. For example, you can use the Forest-based and Boosted Regression and Classification tool to predict the dependent variable using all features in the prediction model. However, the model will assume that the relationships between the variables are consistent across the map. Instead, you can divide the data into regions in which the relationships are as consistent as possible. By building separate models within each region, the tool can more easily learn the local characteristics and relationships, which often results in more accurate predictions and more meaningful interpretation of the relationships. You can separate the regions into separate feature classes using the Split By Attributes tool and providing the region ID field in the Split Fields parameter.", "section": "Usage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html#usage

## 創建本地建模區域(空間統計工具) · 使用率

**id**　73325d70ca8b3c33ce-3

**english**　Create Regions for Local Modeling (SPiRe)

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 模擬空間關系工具軟件

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html

**text**　在某些情況下,返回的區域會比區域數參數中指定的少一些。工具通過確定哪些新區域能提高總體預測準確度,依次構建區域,確定哪個新區域能夠提高整體預測準確性;然而,在一些情況下,沒有新的區域可以提高準確性,而該工具將返回最后一個提供改進的區域的最后數目。在這種情況下,將返回一個警告信息。輸出重要性計分表參數將創建一張表格和圖表,顯示每個創建區域最重要的解釋變量。信息包括每個區域的準確性信息以及顯示每個新區域的準確性提高程度的圖表。至少需要100個輸入功能,每個區域必須包含至少30個特征。使用最小特性數量來提高這一數值。區域參數。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "In some cases, fewer regions will be returned than were specified in the Number of Regions parameter. The tool sequentially builds regions by determining which new region most improves overall prediction accuracy. However, in some cases no new regions will improve accuracy, and the tool will return that last number of regions that provided improvement. In this case, a warning message will be returned.\nThe Output Importance Scores Table parameter creates a table and chart displaying which explanatory variables were most important for each created region.\nThe messages include information about the accuracy of each region as well as a chart displaying how much accuracy improves with each new region.\nAt least 100 input features are required, and at least 30 features must be included in each region. This value can be increased using the Minimum Number of Features per Region parameter.", "section": "Usage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html#usage

## 創建本地建模區域(空間統計工具) · 參數

**id**　73325d70ca8b3c33ce-10

**english**　Create Regions for Local Modeling (SPiRe)

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 模擬空間關系工具軟件

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html

**parameter**　spatial_constraints

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標簽標簽
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 空間限制(備選)
- 指定區域的空間限制。例如,可以要求區域內的所有特征都構成毗連區塊,沒有與該區域其他部分分離的特征。無-將不應用空間限制。區域一般是緊湊的,但可能有一些特征與該區域的其余部分分離。相鄰邊緣——每個區域都是由共同邊或角連接的多邊形毗連區塊。這是多邊形特征的默認值,只適用于多邊形。Trimmmed Delaunay三角測量——同一區域的所有點都將產生一個毗連區塊,其邊緣或角在Delounay三角中具有相同位置。這是點特征的默認值。并且只適用于點。 從文件-空間限制中獲取空間重量, 由指定的空間重量文件(. swm)來定義。 在空間加權矩陣文件參數中提供文件。 您可以使用“ 生成空間重量矩陣工具” 創建文件 。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["Spatial Constraints (Optional)", "Specifies the spatial constraints of the regions. For example, you can require that all features in a region form a contiguous block with no features separated from the rest of the region. None—No spatial constrains will be applied. Regions will generally be compact, but there may be some features that are separated from the rest of the region. Contiguity edges corners—Each region will be a contiguous block of polygons that are connected by a shared edge or corner. This is the default for polygon features and is only applicable for polygons. Trimmed Delaunay triangulation—All points in the same region will create a contiguous block of points that share an edge or corner in their Delaunay triangulation. This is the default for point features and is only applicable for points. Get spatial weights from file—Spatial constraints are defined by a specified spatial weights file (.swm). Provide the file in the Spatial Weights Matrix File parameter. You can create the file using the Generate Spatial Weights Matrix tool.", "String"], "section": "Parameters"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html#parameters

**id**　73325d70ca8b3c33ce-20

**english**　Create Regions for Local Modeling (SPiRe)

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 模擬空間關系工具軟件

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html

**parameter**　spatial_constraints

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 姓名名稱名稱名稱
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 空間限制( 可選)
- 指定區域的空間限制。例如,您可以要求區域內的所有特征都構成一個毗連區塊,沒有與該區域其他部分分離的特征。將不使用非空間限制。區域一般是緊湊的,但可能有一些特點與該區域其他地區分開。CONGUITIEGES_EDGES_CORNERS-每個區域都是由共同邊緣或角連接的多邊形毗連區塊。這是多邊形特性的默認值,僅適用于多邊形。TRIMMED_DELAUNAY_TRINANGULAND-同一區域的所有點都將形成一個毗連區塊,其邊緣或角位于其底勞納。這是點特征的默認值, 只適用于點。 Get_ SPATIAL_ WEIGES_ FROM_ FILE- 空間限制由指定空間重量文件定義(. swm) 。 在 重量_ matrix_ 文件參數中提供文件。 您可以使用“ 生成空間比重矩陣工具” 創建文件 。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["spatial_constraints (Optional)", "Specifies the spatial constraints of the regions. For example, you can require that all features in a region form a contiguous block with no features separated from the rest of the region. NONE—No spatial constrains will be applied. Regions will generally be compact, but there may be some features that are separated from the rest of the region. CONTIGUITY_EDGES_CORNERS—Each region will be a contiguous block of polygons that are connected by a shared edge or corner. This is the default for polygon features and is only applicable for polygons. TRIMMED_DELAUNAY_TRIANGULATION—All points in the same region will create a contiguous block of points that share an edge or corner in their Delaunay triangulation. This is the default for point features and is only applicable for points. GET_SPATIAL_WEIGHTS_FROM_FILE—Spatial constraints are defined by a specified spatial weights file (.swm). Provide the file in the weights_matrix_file parameter. You can create the file using the Generate Spatial Weights Matrix tool.", "String"], "section": "Parameters"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html#parameters

)

## 創建本地建模區域(空間統計工具) · 代碼樣本

**id**　73325d70ca8b3c33ce-27

**english**　Create Regions for Local Modeling (SPiRe)

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 模擬空間關系工具軟件

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html

**text**　# Create six regions predicting sales prices for houses.
# Regions are not required to be contiguous.

import arcpy
arcpy.env.workspace = r"C:\MyData\MyData.gdb"

arcpy.stats.CreateRegionsForLocalModeling(
    in_features="house_sales",
    dependent_variable="sale_price",
    explanatory_variables="square_feet, num_bedrooms, num_bathrooms, age",
    out_features=r"housing_price_regions",
    num_regions=6,
    spatial_constraints="NONE",
    weights_matrix_file=None,
    out_importance_table=r"housing_price_importance",
    min_features_per_region=30
)

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_features", "label_zh": "輸入特性", "label_en": "Input Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The point or polygon features that will be divided into regions."}, {"parameter": "dependent_variable", "label_zh": "依賴變量", "label_en": "Dependent Variable", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field representing the dependent variable. The dependent variable is predicted by the explanatory variables."}, {"parameter": "explanatory_variables", "label_zh": "解釋變量", "label_en": "Explanatory Variables", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The fields of the explanatory variables. The explanatory variables will be used to predict the dependent variable."}, {"parameter": "out_features", "label_zh": "輸出特性", "label_en": "Output Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "The output features of the regions. Each feature will include a region ID indicating which region it is in."}, {"parameter": "num_regions", "label_zh": "區域數目(可選)", "label_en": "Number of Regions (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The number of regions that will be created. If no value is provided, the number will be estimated while the tool runs."}, {"parameter": "spatial_constraints", "label_zh": "空間限制(備選)", "label_en": "Spatial Constraints (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the spatial constraints of the regions. For example, you can require that all features in a region form a contiguous block with no features separated from the rest of the region. None—No spatial constrains will be applied. Regions will generally be compact, but there may be some features that are separated from the rest of the region. Contiguity edges corners—Each region will be a contiguous block of polygons that are connected by a shared edge or corner. This is the default for polygon features and is only applicable for polygons. Trimmed Delaunay triangulation—All points in the same region will create a contiguous block of points that share an edge or corner in their Delaunay triangulation. This is the default for point features and is only applicable for points. Get spatial weights from file—Spatial constraints are defined by a specified spatial weights file (.swm). Provide the file in the Spatial Weights Matrix File parameter. You can create the file using the Generate Spatial Weights Matrix tool."}, {"parameter": "weights_matrix_file", "label_zh": "空間加權矩陣文件( 可選)", "label_en": "Spatial Weights Matrix File (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "File", "description_en": "The path and file name of the spatial weights matrix file that defines the spatial constraints for the regions."}, {"parameter": "out_importance_table", "label_zh": "表(備選)", "label_en": "Output Importance Scores Table (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Table", "description_en": "The output table containing importance scores for all explanatory variables in every region. The output layer will include a chart showing the relative importance of each explanatory variable in each region."}, {"parameter": "min_features_per_region", "label_zh": "各地區最低地物數量(可選)", "label_en": "Minimum Number of Features Per Region (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The minimum number of features that must in each region. All regions will have feature counts greater than or equal to this value. The default is 30, and the value must be at least 30."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.stats.CreateRegionsForLocalModeling(in_features, dependent_variable, explanatory_variables, out_features, {num_regions}, {spatial_constraints}, {weights_matrix_file}, {out_importance_table}, {min_features_per_region})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_features、dependent_variable、explanatory_variables、out_features、num_regions、spatial_constraints、weights_matrix_file、out_importance_table、min_features_per_region。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 創建本地建模區域(空間統計工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 模擬空間關系工具軟件

**english**　Create Regions for Local Modeling (SPiRe)

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html

**id**　73325d70ca8b3c33ce-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Create Regions for Local Modeling (SPiRe)，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入特性 [in_features]、依賴變量 [dependent_variable]、解釋變量 [explanatory_variables]、輸出特性 [out_features]、區域數目(可選) [num_regions]、空間限制(備選) [spatial_constraints]、空間加權矩陣文件( 可選) [weights_matrix_file]、表(備選) [out_importance_table]、各地區最低地物數量(可選) [min_features_per_region]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/create-regions.html#parameters

**text**　修改復本方案使其與關系復本的方案相匹配是一個獨立于資料同步的過程。 可使用以下工具執行此操作： 使用 比較復本方案 工具生成包含方案變更的 .xml 文件。 使用 導入復本方案 工具導入變更。 要應用復本方案變更，請運行 導出復本方案 工具以將包含變更的復本方案導出到 .xml 文件。 然后，將該 .xml 文件用作比較復本方案工具的輸入。
輸出復本方案變更文件必須為 XML 格式。

## 比較復本方案 (資料管理工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　7334687eaadb2076f3-14

**english**　Compare Replica Schema

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 分布式地理資料庫工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/compare-replica-schema.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/compare-replica-schema.html

**text**　# Description: Compare a replica schema (in an enterprise geodatabase
#              workspace) to its relative replicas schema (in an .xml file).
#              The results of the comparison are created in an .xml file.
#              The relative replica's .xml schema file was created using the
#              ExportReplicaSchema function.

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set workspace
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/Data"

# Set local variables
in_geodatabase = "MySDEdata.sde"
in_source_file = "RelativeReplicaSchema.xml"
output_schema_changes = "outputSchemaChanges.xml"

# Run CompareReplicaSchema
arcpy.management.CompareReplicaSchema(in_geodatabase, in_source_file, output_schema_changes)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/compare-replica-schema.html#parameters

## 比較復本方案 (資料管理工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 分布式地理資料庫工具集

**english**　Compare Replica Schema

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/compare-replica-schema.html

**id**　7334687eaadb2076f3-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/compare-replica-schema.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Compare Replica Schema，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。與復本地理資料庫比較 [in_geodatabase]、相關復本方案文件 [in_source_file]、輸出復本方案變更文件 [output_replica_schema_changes_file]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/compare-replica-schema.html#parameters

## 030158：出行模式值無效：<value>。 該值必須是出行模式的有效 JSON 制圖表達，或以下名稱之一：<value>。 · 解決方案

**id**　733d70fe952e667aad-2

**english**　030158: Invalid travel mode value: <value>. The value must be a valid JSON representation of a travel mode or one of the following names:<value>.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 30101-30200

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/030001-040000/tool-errors-and-warnings-30151-30175-030158.html#solution

**text**　如果出行模式使用 JSON 制圖表達指定，則該值必須是有效的 JSON 字符串。
如果出行模式使用名稱指定，則該值必須來自受支持的出行模式名稱列表。

- 標注
- 說明

- 指定像元寬度和高度的測量單位。 米—單位將為米。 這是默認設置。 千米—單位將為千米。 英里—單位將為英里。 海里—單位將為海里。 英尺—單位將為英尺。 美國測量英尺—單位將為美國測量英尺。
- String
